Licensed data, its figures need a licence.<br>**Record**: A count of similar past cases: one played out if price moved a set distance, scaled to its usual swings, the stated way within the period.<br>**Ran out of time**: Within the period price reached the set distance neither way. The claim closed without an outcome. |
